[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_413880148}

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[alarm-detect]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1828877811}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下时，接口的告警联动动作。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo alarm-detect]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_677834803}[命令用来取消告警联动动作。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:宋体;color:windowtext"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_306934281}

[**[alarm-detect]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **rdi** \| **sd** \| **sf** } **action link-down**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x821611488}

[**[undo alarm-detect]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **rdi** \| **sd** \| **sf** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903166715}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:宋体;color:windowtext"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x5514730}

[[接口不执行任何告警联动动作。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_964015611}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:宋体;color:windowtext"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1614587419}

[[Ten-GigabitEthernet]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_40270107}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:宋体;color:windowtext"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1166484352}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903232251}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x746846331}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:宋体;color:windowtext"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1720756686}

[**[rdi]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1257067897}[：表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RDI]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Remote Defect Indication]{lang="EN-US"}[，远端失效指示）告警。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[sd]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1240724683}[：表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Signal Degrade]{lang="EN-US"}[，信号衰减）告警。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[sf]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x426348287}[：表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Signal Fail]{lang="EN-US"}[，信号失败）告警。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[action link-down]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903035643}[：表示当接口检测到告警时，会自动将接口的物理状态设置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:宋体;color:windowtext"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1940010881}

[[当设备收到对端发送的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MS-RDI]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1943159758}[信号时，则认为发生了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RDI]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Remote Defect Indication]{lang="EN-US"}[，远端失效指示）告警。当设备收到的报文的误码率超过设置的门限时，则生成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警的门限可通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[threshold]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令设置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[配置本命令后，当设备检测到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1351114031}[告警]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/RDI]{lang="EN-US"}[告警时，会自动将接口的物理状态设置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:宋体;color:windowtext"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1706667387}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1208818530}[配置当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}[接口检测到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警时，自动将接口的物理状态设置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[接口的期望带宽＝接口的最大速率÷]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_324729880}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbit/s]{lang="EN-US"}[）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1405070587}[以太网子接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1769031870}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_181438413}

[*[bandwidth-value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2115709001}[：表示接口的期望带宽，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[400000000]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbit/s]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x497869910}

[[接口的期望带宽会对下列内容有影响：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_724503861}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[CBQ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1573310247}[队列带宽。具体介绍请参见"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[QoS]{lang="EN-US"}[配置指导"中的"[拥塞管理]{#_Toc263760148}"。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[链路开销值。具体介绍请参见"三层技术]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[-IP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_79181101}[路由配置指导"中的"]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}["、"]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPFv3]{lang="EN-US"}["和"]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x190057309}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- combo enable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x333148142}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1768900798}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[combo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **enable**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_609592212}[命令用来激活]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}[接口中的电口或者光口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1123373719}

[**[combo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **enable** { **copper** \| **fiber** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1939378645}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1374307822}

[[电口被激活。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_347935677}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1708398655}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1988947824}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1945926310}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1768966334}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1620509918}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1656009554}

[**[copper]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x356684503}[：表示该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的电口被激活，请使用双绞线连接。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[fiber]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1405296735}[：表示该]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的光口被激活，请使用光纤连接。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_440798559}

[[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1607838564}[接口是一个逻辑接口，一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}[接口物理上对应设备面板上一个电口和一个光口。电口与其对应的光口是光电复用关系，两者不能同时工作（当激活其中的一个接口时，另一个接口就自动处于关闭状态），用户可根据组网需求选择使用电口或光口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[请根据设备面板上的标识了解设备上有哪些]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1406650598}[接口以及每个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的编号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1790210027}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1769425085}[指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的电口被激活。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x730394305}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] combo enable copper]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x519958555}[指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[端口的光口被激活。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x295929900}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] combo enable fiber]{lang="EN-US"}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- dampening**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_413945684}[命令用来开启接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo dampening]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903166716}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x5449194}

[**[dampening ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ *half-life* *reuse suppress max-suppress-time* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1437250551}

[**[undo dampening]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_226049929}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_436869003}

[[接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903232252}[功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x746911867}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_248481355}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1662651053}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903035644}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1940469633}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1109548230}

[*[half-life]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2134849506}[：半衰期，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[120]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[54]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[reuse]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1902227539}[：启用值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[200]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20000]{lang="EN-US"}[，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[750]{lang="EN-US"}[，必须要小于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[suppress]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[suppress]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903101180}[：抑制门限，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[200]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20000]{lang="EN-US"}[，缺省值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2000]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[max-suppress-time]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1182229874}[：最大抑制时间，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[255]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，缺省值为半衰期的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[倍，即]{style="font-family:宋体"}[162]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1380825224}

[[接口有两种物理连接状态：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_403085156}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[。由于线缆故障、接口连接或链路层配置错误等问题，可能会导致设备接口的状态频繁的在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[之间切换，这种现象称为接口震荡。随着接口状态的频繁改变，设备会不停的刷新相关表项（比如路由表），消耗大量的系统资源。通过在接口上配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}[功能，可以在一定条件下，屏蔽该接口的震荡对路由等上层业务的影响。此时若出现接口震荡，将不上送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[处理，仅产生对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trap]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Log]{lang="EN-US"}[信息，从而节省系统资源的消耗。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[最大惩罚值与最大抑制时间、半衰期、启用值之间遵循公式：最大惩罚值＝]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2^(^]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x688354801}^[最大抑制时间]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[半衰期]{style="font-family:宋体"}[)]{lang="EN-US"}^[×启用值，其中最大惩罚值不可配。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903428860}[功能后，各参数运行情况：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[接口将对应一个惩罚值，初始值是]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x376793536}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。接口状态每次从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[变到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[时，惩罚值会增加]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000]{lang="EN-US"}[（接口状态从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[变到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[时，惩罚值不变）。同时，惩罚值随时间推移自动减少，满足半衰期衰减规律：完全衰减时（假如没有接口震荡），经过一个半衰周期，惩罚值减少为原来值的一半（软件模拟的半衰期，取样时间是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[秒，惩罚值的递减在每个取样定时器中操作。开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}[的同时会创建并激活取样定时器，只有执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo dampening]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令才会删除定时器）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[当惩罚值大于或等于抑制门限时，开始抑制接口：不上送]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1877636928}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[处理接口状态变化，仅产生对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trap]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Log]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。当惩罚值小于或等于启用门限时，不抑制接口：上送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[处理接口状态变化，同时发送对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trap]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Log]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[当惩罚值达到最大惩罚值后，惩罚值将不再增加。每次接口进入抑制状态后，持续抑制的时间超过最大抑制时间时，惩罚值不再增加，此时惩罚值进入完全半衰期（此阶段接口状态变化不会增加惩罚值），直到惩罚值小于启用值，不再抑制接口（完全半衰时，接口仍然处于抑制状态，但完全半衰阶段时间不算入持续抑制时间）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_381022309}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果接口抑制时间不到最大抑制时间，惩罚值就小于启用值，那么不存在完全半衰过程（持续抑制时间超过最大抑制时间才会进入）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1656069783}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903494396}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[以太网接口上不能同时配置本命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_416334805}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[link-delay]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[本命令对使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x918447667}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令手动关闭的接口无效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[手工]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x274380994}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[时，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}[的惩罚值恢复]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[为初始值]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对于使能了]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1096614439}[RRPP]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MSTP]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Smart Link]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[接]{style="font-family:宋体"}[口不建议使用该命令。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[配置该命令时，如果不指定任何参数，开启的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903297788}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}[功能将采用缺省值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1548792183}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2131557131}[按照缺省值开启接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x330796862}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] dampening]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x903363324}[开启接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dampening]{lang="EN-US"}[功能，配置半衰期为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[秒，启用值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[800]{lang="EN-US"}[，抑制门限为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3000]{lang="EN-US"}[，最大抑制时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[接口下的某些配置恢复到缺省情况后，会对设备上当前运行的业务产生影响。建议您在执行该命令前，完全了解其对网络产生的影响。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_430808892}

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x140236795}

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[display counters]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_676484353}[命令用来显示接口的流量统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1193383196}

[**[display counters ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **inbound** \| **outbound** } **interface** \[ *interface-type* \[ *interface-number* \| *interface-number.subnumber* \] \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2092762376}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x958664126}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1890066525}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2056175131}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1990271611}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1769031869}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1641629398}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_379346191}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1408490516}

[**[inbound]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2125538149}[：显示输入报文的流量统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[outbound]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1703031714}[：显示输出报文的流量统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2063937349}[：指定接口类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1146128837}[：指定接口编号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number.subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1188027673}[：指定子接口。其中]{style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为主接口编号；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*[为子接口编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1769097405}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[本命令显示的是统计周期内报文的数量，统计周期可以通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_280414568}[命令进行设置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[可通过命令]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[reset counters interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1621909015}[清除以太网接口的统计信息，具体指导请参见]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[reset counters interface]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令描述]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1682158550}[，则显示所有可统计的接口的流量统计信息]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_377718101}[而不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[/*interface-number.subnumber*]{lang="EN-US"}[，则显示该类型下所有接口的流量统计信息]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果同时指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1853835319}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[/*interface-number.subnumber*]{lang="EN-US"}[，则显示指定接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[子接口的报文流量统计信息。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x604026550}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x462628432}[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ethernet]{lang="EN-US"}[类型接口的报文输入流量统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_813132107}[：指定接口编号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number.subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1677280661}[：指定子接口编号。其中]{style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为主接口编号；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*[为子接口编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_577795124}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2030677596}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则显示所有可统计的接口类型中最近一个统计周期内处于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[接口的报文速率统计信息]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1856918780}[而不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则显示该类型下最近一个统计周期内处于]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态接口的报文速率统计信息]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果同时指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x203078997}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则显示]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[最近一个统计周期内报文速率统计信息。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[如果该接口在最近一个统计周期内一直处于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[状态，则提示接口不支持该操作。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[关于统计周期的值，分为以下几种情况：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1140888909}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[不支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x171055194}[命令的设备，统计周期]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[固定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[分钟。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对于支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2023513955}[命令的设备，统计周期可以通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}**[flow-interval]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令来]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_245466454}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1222854250}[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet]{lang="EN-US"}[类型接口的报文接收速率统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[在最近一个统计周期内，接口接收或发送所有类型报文的平均速率（单位为包]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x104620267}[秒）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Broadcast (pps)]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x202947925}

[[在最近一个统计周期内，接口接收或发送广播报文的平均速率（单位为包]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x23375978}[秒）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[物理端口不对广播报文单独进行统计，而是与组播报文一起都按照组播报文进行统计]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Multicast (pps)]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1341592315}

[[在最近一个统计周期内，接口接收或发送组播报文的平均速率（单位为包]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_475355646}[秒）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RPR]{lang="EN-US"}[物理端口不对广播报文单独进行统计，而是与组播报文一起都按照组播报文进行统计]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\--: Not supported.]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x203013461}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- display ethernet statistics**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ethernet statistics]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1058570127}[命令用来显示以太网软件模块收发报文的统计信息。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x376363924}

[[集中式设备：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x202816853}

[**[display ethernet statistics]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x789062889}

[[分布式设备－独立运行模式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x391947577}[集中式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[设备：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[display ethernet statistics]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **slot** *slot-number* \[ **cpu** v*cpu-numbe* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x916612320}

[[分布式设备－]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x887232089}[模式：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[display ethernet statistics chassis ]{lang="EN-US"}***[chassis-number ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot]{lang="EN-US"}***[ slot-number ]{lang="EN-US"}*[\[ **cpu** v*cpu-numbe* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1363584246}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x64299078}

[[用户视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_667488698}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:
黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1168419}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x202882389}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1120929976}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1433908177}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_321979046}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2018245832}

[**[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1017752014}[：显示指定单板的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示单板所在的槽位号。（分布式设备－独立运行模式）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x382418999}[：显示指定成员设备的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号。（集中式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[设备）（不支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1240569396}[：显示指定成员设备或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[的虚拟槽位号。（集中式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[设备）（支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[chassis ]{lang="EN-US"}***[chassis-number ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1923768295}[：显示指定成员设备上指定单板的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[chassis-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示单板所在的槽位号。（分布式设备－]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[模式）（不支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[chassis ]{lang="EN-US"}***[chassis-number ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x511091537}[：显示指定单板]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[chassis-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的虚拟槽位号，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示单板]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[所在的槽位号。（分布式设备－]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[模式）（不支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[cpu]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *cpu-number*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_654842967}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[cpu-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号。只有指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[slot]{lang="EN-US"}[支持多]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[时，才能配置该参数。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- display interface**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1013088362}[命令用来显示指定接口当前的运行状态和相关信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x203144534}

[**[display interface]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ *interface-type* \[ *interface-number \| interface-number.subnumber* \] \] \[ **brief** \[ **description** \| **down** \] \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_768442878}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_187086887}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1488416980}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_671607621}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1844260652}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1336361985}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x987914085}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x202947926}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x23179370}

[*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1107056465}[：指定接口类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1214909734}[：指定接口编号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number.subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1499935802}[：指定子接口编号。其中]{style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为主接口编号；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*[为子接口编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[brief]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2037298519}[：显示接口的概要信息。不指定该参数时，将显示接口的详细信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2075933819}[：用来显示用户配置的接口的全部描述信息。如果某接口的描述信息超过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[27]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符，不指定该参数时，只显示描述信息中的前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[27]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符，超出部分不显示；指定该参数时，可以显示全部描述信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[down]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2073647168}[：显示当前物理状态为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的接口的信息以及]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的原因。不指定该参数时，将不会根据接口物理状态来过滤显示信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x203013462}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果不指定接口类型和接口编号，则显示所有接口的信息；]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1602782239}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果仅指定接口类型，则显示所有该类型接口的信息；]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_747158134}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果同时指定接口类型和接口编号，则显示指定接口的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x612793}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_427529746}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1700698285}[查看三层以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的运行状态和相关信息。（本命令的显示信息与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[描述]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x708974354}

[[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_187417645}

[[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_892989583}[的相关信息]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Current state]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x700714849}

[[接口的物理状态，状态可能为：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x203210072}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Administratively DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_361427129}[：表示该接口已经通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
  宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令被关闭，即管理状态为关闭]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2061600718}[：表示该接口的管理状态为开启，但物理状态为关闭（可能因为没有物理连线或者线路故障）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[UP(spoofing)]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x626436089}[：表示该接口的数据链路层协议状态为开启，但实际可能没有对应的链路，或者所对应的链路不是永久存在而是按需建立。通常]{style="font-family:宋体"}[NULL]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LoopBack]{lang="EN-US"}[等接口会具有该属性]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1613621207}[：表示数据链路层协议状态为关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[DOWN(*protocol*)]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x623865414}[：表示接口的数据链路层被一个或者多个协议模块关闭。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[protocol]{lang="EN-US"}*[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DLDP]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OAM]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LAGG]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BFD]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MACSEC]{lang="EN-US"}[的任意组合，例如，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN(DLDP)]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN(DLDP, OAM)]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN(DLDP, OAM, LAGG)]{lang="EN-US"}[等：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[¡[ ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:5.0pt;font-family:Wingdings"}[当]{style="font-family:

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果某接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1177295581}[Link]{lang="EN-US"}[属性值为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ADM]{lang="EN-US"}["，则表示该接口被管理员手工关闭了，需要在该接口下执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令才能恢复接口本身的物理状态]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1709932377}[：表示接口的数据链路层不通]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[UP(s)]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1709211481}[：表示接口的数据链路层协议状态显示为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[UP]{lang="EN-US"}[，但实际可能没有对应的链路，或者对应的链路不是永久存在而是按需建立的。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[通常]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[NULL]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[LoopBack]{lang="EN-US"}[等接口会]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[取该值]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Main IP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_475428728}

[[接口主]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366140555}[地址。当显示"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[\--]{lang="EN-US"}["时，表示接口下还没有配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Description]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2053389359}

[[用户通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1166484075}[命令给接口配置的描述信息。使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[display interface brief]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数时，该字段最多显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[27]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符；指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[description]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数时，可显示配置的全部描述信息]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[The brief information of interface(s) under bridge mode:]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1509762481}

[[二层模式下（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[bridge]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x769705382}[）的接口概要信息，即二层接口的概要信息]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[Speed or Duplex: (a)/A - auto; H - half; F - full]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366206091}

[[如果某接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Speed]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1085753818}[属性值为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[(a)]{lang="EN-US"}["，则表示该接口的速率是通过自动协商获取的]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果某接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Duplex]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_532236363}[属性值为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[(a)]{lang="EN-US"}["或者"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[A]{lang="EN-US"}["，则表示该接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Duplex]{lang="EN-US"}[属性是通过自动协商获取的；取值为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[H]{lang="EN-US"}["则表示为半双工；取值为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[F]{lang="EN-US"}["则表示为全双工]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[A]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x739241122}[：表示双工模式由自动协商结果决定]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[H]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1257143822}[：表示半双工]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[接口物理连接状态为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1613271092}[的原因，取值为（不同型号的设备支持的取值不同，请以设备的实际情况为准）：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Administratively]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1335717692}[：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
  宋体"}[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[本链路被手工关闭了（配置了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令），需要执行]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令才能恢复真实的物理状态]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[DOWN ( Link-Aggregation interface down )]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1006627670}[：聚合接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[被关闭后，该聚合接口的所有成员端口的状态会显示为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的原因会显示为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN ( Link-Aggregation interface down )]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[DOWN (Loopback detection down)]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365616267}[：由于环路检测模块检测到环路而自动关闭接口]{style="font-family:
  宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[DOWN ( Monitor-Link uplink down )]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2041284256}[：由于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Monitor Link]{lang="EN-US"}[模块检测到上行链路]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[而自动关闭接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[IRF-link-down]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1651504304}[：当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[链路检测功能检测到某成员设备上某]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MDC]{lang="EN-US"}[中的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[链路状态为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}[时，会将该成员设备上这个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MDC]{lang="EN-US"}[中除了保留接口外的所有物理接口状态设置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的原因会显示为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF-link-down]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[MAD ShutDown]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365681803}[：当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[分裂后，处于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Recovery]{lang="EN-US"}[状态的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[会将除了保留接口外的所有接口状态设置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的原因会显示为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAD ShutDown]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Not connected]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x411199237}[：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[没有物理连接（可能没有插网线或者网线故障）]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Storm-Constrain]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x752156074}[：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
  宋体"}[表示端口上因为未知单播、组播或广播报文中某类报文的流量大于其上限阈值而被关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[STP DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366140554}[：由于触发了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[STP BPDU]{lang="EN-US"}[保护而自动关闭接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Port Security Disabled]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_675493996}[：因检测到端口收到非法报文，端口安全的入侵检测机制将端口关闭]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[Standby]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1481557903}[：表示接口处于备份状态]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令的支持情况与接口的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1028480298}

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[display packet-drop]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_174535523}[命令用来显示接口丢弃的报文的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_343139876}

[**[display packet-drop]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **interface** \[ *interface-type* \[ *interface-number* \] \] \| **summary** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1408323318}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1064218266}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x911047095}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366009482}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_247141371}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_416980390}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x632513742}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_703813394}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x120754163}

[*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1039454451}[：显示指定类型接口丢弃的报文的信息。不指定该参数时，显示所有接口丢弃的报文的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1794614}[：显示指定编号接口丢弃的报文的信息。不指定该参数时，显示该类型所有接口丢弃的报文的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[summary]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366075018}[：将所有接口丢弃报文的统计信息累计后再显示。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1107811944}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1549363327}[显示接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[丢弃报文的信息。（本命令显示信息的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- duplex**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[duplex]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1794477965}[命令用来设置以太网接口的双工模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo duplex]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1627843695}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1210085199}

[**[duplex]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **auto** \| **full** \| **half** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x255798583}

[**[undo duplex]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365616266}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x475200315}

[[以太网接口的双工模式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1011023396}[（自协商）状态，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE/40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的双工模式为全双工状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1032222543}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x818479465}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x486669600}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1755649263}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1123330811}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_489242889}

[**[auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365681802}[：接口与对端接口自动协商双工状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[full]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1977283178}[：全双工状态，接口在发送数据包的同时可以接收数据包。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[half]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x761921643}[：半双工状态，接口同一时刻只能发送数据包或接收数据包。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1095734847}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1127426748}[将以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[接口设置为全双工状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_200100386}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] duplex full]{lang="EN-US"}
:::

[]{#_Toc404783549}[]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_272147156}[]{#_Toc309912018}[]{#_Toc282617160}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- eee enable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image003.png){width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1074488193}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[设备是否支持本特性以及哪些类型的接口支持本特性与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366140557}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[eee enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x890589945}[命令用来使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[EEE]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Energy Efficient Ethernet]{lang="EN-US"}[）节能功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo eee enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1549864087}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1732611991}

[**[eee enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1739817777}

[**[undo eee enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x857118960}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1829934808}

[[EEE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1059414093}[节能功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_51860008}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366206093}

[[【缺省级别】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_77045596}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1259524905}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x143586289}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1672798141}

[[接口使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[EEE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1037416693}[节能功能后，如果在连续一段时间（由芯片规格决定，不能通过命令行配置）内接口状态始终为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[且没有收发任何报文，则接口自动进入低功耗模式；当接口需要收发报文时，接口又自动恢复到正常工作模式，从而达到节能的效果。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x248108184}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366009485}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[下]{style="font-family:宋体"}[使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[EEE]{lang="EN-US"}[节能功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1318942570}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] eee enable]{lang="EN-US"}
:::::

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- flag sdh**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image003.png){#图片 5 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_980587740}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[·[          ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1623302219}

[[·[          ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}[本命令只有]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_576724279}[接口工作在]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下时配置才有效。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[flag sdh]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1517235272}[命令用来设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SDH]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Synchronous Digital Hierarchy]{lang="EN-US"}[，同步数字系列）帧开销字段中]{style="font-family:宋体"}[J0]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[J1]{lang="EN-US"}[字节的值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo flag sdh]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1062822714}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366075021}

[**[flag ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **j0** \| **j1** } **sdh** *value*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x814698965}

[**[undo flag ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **j0** \| **j1** } **sdh**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1316456519}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_517924159}

[[J0]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_338145201}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[J1]{lang="EN-US"}[字节的值为全]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1280804413}

[[Ten-GigabitEthernet]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x867940139}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1655430744}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_855419769}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365878413}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x254032744}

[**[j0]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2146895979}[：再生段踪迹字节。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[j1]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_672848003}[：通道踪迹字节。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[value]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x50195085}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[J0]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[J1]{lang="EN-US"}[字节的值，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[15]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1782713919}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_845993975}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SDH]{lang="EN-US"}[帧中]{style="font-family:宋体"}[J0]{lang="EN-US"}[字节的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Sysname]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365943949}

[\[Sysname\] interface ten-gigabitethernet 1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1\] port-mode wan]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1\] flag j0 sdh Sysname]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1877161149}

margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image003.png){#图片 1 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1027152910}

margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1102388472}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}[只有本端和对端设备都开启了流量控制功能，才能实现对本端以太网接口的流量控制。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x913323804}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_973960299}[命令用来开启以太网接口的流量控制功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1920984145}[命令用来关闭以太网接口流量控制功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365747341}

[**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x669805096}

[**[undo flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1124165847}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1631922700}

[[以太网接口的流量控制功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2044647971}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x533466989}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_309393764}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1119501405}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_324684222}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365812877}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1518896790}

[[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x963211176}[命令后，设备具有发送和接收流量控制报文的能力：当本端发生拥塞时，设备会向对端发送流量控制报文；当本端收到对端的流量控制报文后，会停止报文发送。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_337679670}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1276123062}[开启以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的流量控制功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1414547686}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- flow-control receive enable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image003.png){width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x6884520}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365616269}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1590945562}[命令用来配置以太网接口的接收流量控制功能。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[undo flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1694218895}[命令用来关闭以太网接口接收流量控制功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1532277775}

[**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1679837298}

[**[undo flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x293938804}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1956740686}

[[以太网接口的接收流量控制功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_557846647}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x743710673}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365681805}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_751600177}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1820227334}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1108951164}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:
黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x83762}

[[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x852269478}[命令后，设备具有接收流量控制报文的能力，但不具有发送流量控制报文的能力。当设备收到对端的流量控制报文，会停止向对端发送报文；当本端发生拥塞时，设备不能向对端发送流量控制报文。因此，如果要应对单向网络拥塞的情况，可以在一端配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[，在对端配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**[；如果要求本端和对端网络拥塞都能处理，则两端都必须配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2103321117}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_691047264}[使能以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的接收流量控制功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366140556}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[flow-interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1232614661}[命令用来配置接口统计报文信息的时间间隔。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令的缺省情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_137889229}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1636539591}[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interval]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1749866200}[：接口统计信息的时间间隔值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[300]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒，步长为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[（即取值必须为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[的整数倍）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[用户可以通过全局配置（系统视图下）和接口下（以太网接口视图或端口组视图下）的配置来配置以太网接口统计信息的时间间隔：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1141033455}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[link-delay]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1094612431}[命令用来配置以太网接口物理连接状态抑制功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo link-delay]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x405585427}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1988769864}

[**[link-delay ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **msec** \] *delay-time* \[ **mode** { **up** \| **updown** } \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x996129062}

[**[undo link-delay ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **msec** \] *delay-time* \[ **mode** { **up** \| **updown** } \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365681804}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x814483764}

[[本命令的缺省情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_636384466}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2020280761}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_346498839}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_437298516}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1741716618}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x545683503}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366140559}

[**[msec]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x143979507}[：表示配置的抑制时间为毫秒级。不指定该参数时，表示配置的抑制时间为秒级。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[delay-time]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_628439829}[：接口物理连接状态抑制时间值：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x744366641}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[30]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[当和]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x42838682}**[msec]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数一起使用的时候，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000]{lang="EN-US"}[，且为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[的倍数，单位为毫秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_634263491}[表示不抑制，即接口状态改变时立即上报]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mode up]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1644737657}[：设置以太网接口物理连接]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态抑制功能。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mode updown]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1377174424}[：设置以太网接口物理连接]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[状态抑制功能。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x272865535}

[[使用该命令时，选取的参数不同，抑制效果不同：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x143127539}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1202610389}**[mode]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数：表示接口状态从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[变成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[时，不会立即上报]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[。而是等待]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[delay-time]{lang="EN-US"}*[时间后，再检查接口状态，如果状态仍然是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[，再上报。接口状态从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[变成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[时，立即上报]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mode up]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1996872784}[：表示接口状态从]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[变成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[时，不会立即上报]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[。而是等待]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[delay-time]{lang="EN-US"}*[时间后，再检查接口状态，如果状态仍然是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[，再上报。接口状态从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[变成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[时，立即上报]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[mode updown]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_163329256}[：表示接口状态从]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[变成]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[或者]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[变成]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[时，都不会立即上报]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[。等待]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[delay-time]{lang="EN-US"}*[时间后，再检查接口状态，如果状态仍然是]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[或者]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[，再上报。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[同一接口下，接口状态从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_670301844}[变成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[的抑制时间和接口状态从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[变成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[的抑制时间可以不同。如果在同一端口下，多次执行本命令配置了不同的抑制时间，则两个抑制时间会分别以最新配置为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[对于使能了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RRPP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366206095}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MSTP]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Smart Link]{lang="EN-US"}[的端口不推荐使用该命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1239845010}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1750715659}[设置以太网接口物理连接]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[状态抑制时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[8]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1938415011}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] link-delay 8]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x143979508}[设置以太网接口物理连接]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态抑制时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[800]{lang="EN-US"}[毫秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:8.5pt;
font-family:\"Courier New\""}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1232874278}

[[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x143782900}

[[\[Sysname-]{lang="EN-US"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] link-delay msec 800 mode up]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x143127540}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- loopback**

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1105070611}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x320052943}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[loopback]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_793897382}[命令用来对以太网接口进行环回测试。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo loopback]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366009487}[命令用来取消环回测试。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x156143156}

[**[loopback]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **external** \| **internal** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x774663118}

[**[undo loopback]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_561870684}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_125754881}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x782710457}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_867583247}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1561249582}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2058694541}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366075023}

[**[external]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1977498379}[：对以太网接口进行对外环回测试。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[internal]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2067219831}[：对以太网接口进行对内环回测试。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x200010037}

[[在进行某些特殊功能测试时，例如初步定位以太网故障时，需要对以太网接口进行环回测试。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x690032513}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x996787189}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对以太网接口进行环回测试时，接口将不能正常转发数据包。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_896178923}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[手工关闭]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1842266862}[以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[（接口状态显示为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[ADM]{lang="EN-US"}[或者]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Administratively DOWN]{lang="EN-US"}[）时，则不能进行内部和外部环回测试。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[在进行环回测试时系统将禁止在接口上进行]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1068647249}**[speed]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[duplex]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mdix-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令的配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[以太网接口进行环回测试时将工作在全双工状态，环回测试结束后恢复原有配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365878415}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1416832158}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_617887277}[配置以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[进行对内环回测试。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x846986817}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] loopback internal]{lang="EN-US"}

[[配置该命令后，如果在连续一段时间（由芯片规格决定，不能通过命令行配置）内接口状态始终为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_28148900}[，则系统会自动停止对该接口供电，接口自动进入节能模式；当接口状态变为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[时，系统会自动恢复对该接口供电，接口自动进入正常模式，从而达到节能的效果。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1068558096}

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[port link-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1138209905}[命令用来切换以太网接口的工作模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port link-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1661946344}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_504368714}

[**[port link-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **bridge** \| **route** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1118039189}

[**[undo port link-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1913533786}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_549430969}

[[不同型号的业务板上接口的工作模式不同，请以实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365616271}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1234649666}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2124974439}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_866125354}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x240596117}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1873237390}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x500663225}

[**[bridge]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_155811078}[：工作在二层模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[route]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2008603415}[：工作在三层模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365681807}

[[基于业务板的硬件构造，设备上的某些接口只能作为二层以太网接口；某些接口只能作为三层以太网接口；某些接口比较灵活，工作模式可以通过命令行设置。如果将工作模式设置为二层模式（]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[bridge]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1914399591}[），则作为一个二层以太网接口使用，如果将工作模式设置为三层模式（]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[route]{lang="EN-US"}**[），则作为一个三层以太网接口使用。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是，接口模式切换后，除了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1965279874}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[combo enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，该以太网接口下的其它所有命令都将恢复到新模式下的缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_390314657}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_375325830}[使接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[工作在二层模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1771060956}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port link-mode bridge]{lang="EN-US"}

:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[port-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x937644112}[命令用来设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1973227624}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1900053426}

[**[port-mode ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **lan** \| **wan** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1288715873}

[**[undo port-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1259257375}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1652951785}

[[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1190156223}[接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x426491454}

[[Ten-GigabitEthernet]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366206094}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x326238931}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_777823627}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x523577625}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1683983519}

[**[lan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1694694994}[：指定接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。工作在该模式下的接口传输以太网报文，用于连接以太网。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[wan]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1413486797}[：指定接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。工作在该模式下的接口传输]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SDH]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Synchronous Digital Hierarchy]{lang="EN-US"}[，同步数字系列）报文，用于连接]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SDH]{lang="EN-US"}[网络。接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下仅支持点到点的报文传输。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1862402908}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_825420883}[设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}[接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366009486}

[\[Sysname\] interface ten-gigabitethernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1\] port-mode wan]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2003754381}

font-family:Symbol"}[本命令支持的视图与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_100329902}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[priority-flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x721797731}[命令用来配置开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Priority-based Flow Control]{lang="EN-US"}[，基于优先级的流量控制）功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo priority-flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1825796900}[命令用来关闭]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_925941384}

[**[priority-flow-control ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **auto** \| **enable** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1366075022}

[**[undo priority-flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x411414438}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1479564022}

[[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x27334061}[功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1191117343}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_358272301}[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x946406794}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x23571021}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2034361015}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365878414}

[**[auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_149251783}[：表示与对端自动协商是否开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_198626468}[：表示强制开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2020718056}

[[如果本端和对端设备的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1381439695}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Priority-based Flow Control]{lang="EN-US"}[，基于优先级的流量控制）功能处于使能状态，并配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[priority-flow-control no-drop dot1p ]{lang="EN-US"}***[dot1p-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[命令，则当本端收到的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级在]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[dot1p-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[范围内的报文发生拥塞时，会通知对端设备暂时停止向本端发送对应优先级的报文；拥塞解除后，再通知对端继续发送对应优先级的报文。从而保证本设备在转发]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级在]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[dot1p-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[范围内的报文时不丢包。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1392993046}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365616270}[在以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能的开启模式为自动协商模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_331434275}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] priority-flow-control auto]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1789717751}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[priority-flow-control no-drop dot1p]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1737522650}

margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_973487670}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;
font-family:Symbol"}[本命令支持的视图与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1913670260}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[priority-flow-control no-drop dot1p]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365681806}[命令用来开启指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo priority-flow-control no-drop dot1p]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_348315650}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1923393529}

[**[priority-flow-control no-drop dot1p ]{lang="EN-US"}***[dot1p-list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1744247004}

[**[undo priority-flow-control no-drop dot1p]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x643268664}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_265270653}

[[所有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1884136239}[优先级的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能都处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1747041861}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556173746}[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_52982701}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1943975826}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x18986218}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_475487296}

[*[dot1p-]{lang="EN-US"}[list]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x280353427}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CoS]{lang="EN-US"}[值，又称为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[dot1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级）列表，例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1,3-5]{lang="EN-US"}[。（表示数值区间时使用连字符"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[-]{lang="EN-US"}["，数值之间用英文格式的逗号"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[,]{lang="EN-US"}["分隔，最多可配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[16]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1638543376}

[[如果本端和对端设备的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1583730812}[功能处于使能状态，并配置了本命令，那么，当网络发生拥塞时，如果本端设备收到的报文的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级在]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[dot1p-list]{lang="EN-US"}*[范围内，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[则优先发送该报文。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x651433510}[功能处于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[enabled]{lang="EN-US"}[状态时又配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[相应配置优先生效，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[的配置将被忽略；当]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能处于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[disabled]{lang="EN-US"}[状态时又配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[配置生效。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556108210}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1994992637}[在以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能的开启模式为自动协商模式，并开启]{style="font-family:宋体"}[802.1p]{lang="EN-US"}[优先级]{style="font-family:宋体"}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PFC]{lang="EN-US"}[功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x921801137}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] priority-flow-control auto]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] priority-flow-control no-drop dot1p 5]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[flow-control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_234107908}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[flow-control receive enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1889447744}
