**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- reset counters interface**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[reset counters interface]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556304818}[命令用来清除接口的统计信息。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_769695223}

[**[reset counters interface]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ *interface-type* \[ *interface-number* \| *interface-number.subnumber* \] \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1379903786}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2117002881}

[[用户视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1792274586}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1371294098}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1648425044}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1059059517}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_432002770}

[*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556239282}[：指定接口类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2009060322}[：指定接口编号。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[interface-number.subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1758935625}[：指定子接口。其中]{style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[为主接口编号；]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[subnumber]{lang="EN-US"}*[为子接口编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[4094]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x763553449}

[[在某些情况下，需要统计一定时间内某接口的流量，这就需要在统计开始前清除该接口原有的统计信息，重新进行统计。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x630751495}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_856321569}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则清除所有接口的统计信息；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_300659876}[而不指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则清除所有该类型接口的统计信息；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果同时指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x213466973}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}*[interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[，则清除指定接口的统计信息。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1042170920}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556435890}[清除以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[reset ethernet statistics]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x636619856}[命令用来清除以太网软件模块收发报文的统计信息。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556370354}

[[集中式设备：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1478664515}

[**[reset ethernet statistics]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_636618544}

[[分布式设备－独立运行模式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_562134257}[集中式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[设备：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[reset ethernet statistics ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **slot** *slot-number* \[ **cpu** *cpu-number* \] \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1887964205}

[[分布式设备－]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1728661687}[模式：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[reset ethernet statistics ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **chassis** *chassis-number* **slot** *slot-number* \[ **cpu** *cpu-number* \] \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1497933888}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1333587122}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x812934990}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_363701623}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_89203553}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1137887004}

[**[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2054965694}[：清除指定单板的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示单板所在的槽位号。不指定该参数时，表示所有单板。（分布式设备－独立运行模式）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_338883990}[：清除指定成员设备的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号。不指定该参数时，表示所有成员设备。（集中式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[设备）（不支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1623556246}[：表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[的虚拟槽位号。不指定该参数时，表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的所有成员设备]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[。（集中式]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[设备）（支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[chassis ]{lang="EN-US"}***[chassis-number ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_698760523}[：清除指定成员设备上指定单板的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[chassis-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示单板所在的槽位号。不指定该参数时，表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的所有单板。（分布式设备－]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[模式）（不支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[chassis ]{lang="EN-US"}***[chassis-number ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot ]{lang="EN-US"}***[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2145468735}[：清除指定单板]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[的统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[chassis-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示设备在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的成员编号或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[对应的虚拟框号，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[slot-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示单板]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[所在的槽位号。不指定该参数时，表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[中的所有单板]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/PEX]{lang="EN-US"}[。（分布式设备－]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF]{lang="EN-US"}[模式）（支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IRF3]{lang="EN-US"}[的设备）]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[cpu]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *cpu-number*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_608444164}[：清除指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[统计信息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[cpu-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号。只有指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[slot]{lang="EN-US"}[支持多]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CPU]{lang="EN-US"}[时，才能配置该参数。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556501426}

[*[interface-type]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1759796264}[：清除指定类型接口丢弃的报文的信息。不指定该参数时，清除所有接口丢弃的报文的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[10]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1144683487}[：表示接口速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[100]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1566072829}[：表示接口速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[1000]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x218938923}[：表示接口速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，本参数的支持情况与业务板的型号有关，请以业务板的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[10000]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1948940248}[：表示接口速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，本参数的支持情况与业务板的型号有关，请以业务板的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[20000]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_574122803}[：表示接口速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，本参数的支持情况与业务板的型号有关，请以业务板的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[40000]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_187581107}[：表示接口速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，本参数的支持情况与业务板的型号有关，请以业务板的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[100000]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x997015703}[：表示接口速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，本参数的支持情况与业务板的型号有关，请以业务板的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556370355}[：表示接口速率处于自协商状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1478664514}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[对于以太网电口来说，使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1829517135}**[speed]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令设置端口速率，目的是使其与对端进行速率匹配；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**以太网接口 \-- 以太网接口通用配置命令 \-- threshold**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image003.png){#图片 4 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1486079300}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x481641705}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[threshold]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556632499}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[WAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下时，接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限和（或）]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo threshold]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2051741021}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1759730728}

[**[threshold]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **sd** *sdvalue* \| **sf** *sfvalue* } \*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1108684242}

[**[undo threshold]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **sd** \| **sf** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2060517802}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x541632569}

[[本命令的缺省情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x794089381}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2098858949}

[[Ten-GigabitEthernet]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x375151024}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556173744}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_52982703}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1968654446}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1850942962}

[**[sd]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_329136822}[：表示配置的是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Signal Degrade]{lang="EN-US"}[，信号衰减）告警门限。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[sdvalue]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x322327824}[：以]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10e-*sdvalue*]{lang="EN-US"}[的形式表示的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[9]{lang="EN-US"}[。值越大表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限越小。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[sf]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x880374690}[：表示配置的是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Signal Fail]{lang="EN-US"}[，信号失败）告警门限。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[sfvalue]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x616687874}[：以]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10e-*sfvalue*]{lang="EN-US"}[的形式表示的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限值，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[9]{lang="EN-US"}[。值越大表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限越小。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1985656964}

[[SD]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556108208}[告警和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警都用于标识当前线路的性能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警比]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警严重，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[的误码率门限通常比]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[的误码率门限高，即：当出现少量误码时，设备先产生]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警；而当误码率增大到一定程度、说明线路质量严重下降时，设备才产生]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警。因此，应使]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[的告警门限小于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[的告警门限，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[sdvalue]{lang="EN-US"}*[的值应大于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[sfvalue]{lang="EN-US"}*[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_38677509}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1001831472}[配置接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10e-5]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x673143289}

[\[Sysname\] interface ten-gigabitethernet 1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1\] port-mode wan]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1\] threshold sd 5]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1405258098}[配置接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SD]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10e-7]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[SF]{lang="EN-US"}[告警门限为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10e-5]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1424986233}

[\[Sysname\] interface ten-gigabitethernet 1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1\] port-mode wan]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1\] threshold sd 7 sf 5]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_235809428}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556304816}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x7770030}

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_395514497}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[using fortygige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_576411350}[命令用来将从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口拆分的四个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口合并为一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口，或者将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口切换成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo using fortygige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1199874169}[命令用来取消对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的合并，或者取消对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的切换。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_970140183}

[**[using fortygige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_796964267}

[**[undo using fortygige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1853629011}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x635939573}

[[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1718358833}[接口作为单个接口使用，未拆分；]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口未切换成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556239280}

[[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2009060320}[拆分接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x596136211}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_868880086}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_213225992}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1043122029}

[[如果用户需要更大的带宽，可以将已拆分的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1716142471}[接口合并为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口使用。在任何一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[拆分接口下执行该命令均生效，无需在其它拆分接口上配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[如果对端连接的是]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2139747995}[接口，或者用户当前只有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[光模块没有]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[光模块，可以将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口切换成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口使用。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x507633091}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行本命令后，是否需要重启设备]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2139485851}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[业务板才能生效，与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[只有缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1060609562}[MDC]{lang="EN-US"}[上支持该命令，非缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MDC]{lang="EN-US"}[上不支持该命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x797839268}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[using hundredgige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1644133718}[命令用来将从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口拆分出来的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[个或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[12]{lang="EN-US"}[个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口合并为一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口，或者将由]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口切换成的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口恢复成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo using hundredgige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_129852563}[命令用来取消对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[个或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[12]{lang="EN-US"}[个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[拆分接口的合并，或者取消]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的切换。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1272662566}

[**[using hundredgige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x478259803}

[**[undo using hundredgige]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x521475990}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556566960}

[[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1333587120}[拆分接口单独使用，不会合并；切换成的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口不会切换回]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1975734404}

[[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1709476153}[拆分接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[切换接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x191034216}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1996949588}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1124182145}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1052997324}

[[100GE]{lang="EN-US" style="color:black"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556501424}[接口根据不同规格的接口芯片可以被拆分为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[个或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[12]{lang="EN-US"}[个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US" style="color:black"}[接口。不]{style="font-family:宋体;color:black"}[存在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[冗余带宽的]{style="font-family:宋体;color:black"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US" style="color:black"}[接口可以被拆分为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US" style="color:black"}[接口，]{style="font-family:宋体;color:black"}[存在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[20GE]{lang="EN-US" style="color:black"}[冗余带宽的]{style="font-family:宋体;color:black"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US" style="color:black"}[接口可以被拆分为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[12]{lang="EN-US"}[个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US" style="color:black"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体;color:black"}

[[如果用户需要更大的带宽，可以]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2134512943}[将从]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口拆分出来的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[个或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[12]{lang="EN-US"}[个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口合并为一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口使用。在任何一个]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[拆分接口下执行该命令均生效，无需在其它拆分接口上配]{style="font-family:宋体"}[置。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[如果用户需要更大的带宽，可以将]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2139485849}[切换成的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口恢复成]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口使用。对于普通]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口（非]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口切换成的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口），执行本命令会提示不支持。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1527189001}

font-family:Symbol"}[只有缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1679583822}[MDC]{lang="EN-US"}[上支持该命令，非缺省]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MDC]{lang="EN-US"}[上不支持该命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[为了提高端口密度，减少用户使用成本，增加组网灵活性，设备支持将一个高带宽的接口拆分成多个]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x380022297}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口使用。]{style="font-family:宋体"}[例如：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[4]{lang="EN-US"}[0GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_106453703}[接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[FortyGigE1/]{lang="EN-US"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[/49]{lang="EN-US"}[可以拆分成四个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/]{lang="EN-US"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[/49:1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/]{lang="EN-US"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[/49:4]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[100GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1060143973}[接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[HundredGigE5/0/50]{lang="EN-US"}[可以拆分成十个或十二个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet5/0/50:1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet5/0/50:10]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[ Ten-GigabitEthernet5/0/50:1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet5/0/50:12]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[]{#_Toc207010336}[]{#_Toc207010069}[]{#_Toc139515334}[]{#_Toc137103167}[]{#_Toc404783575}[]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556239281}[]{#_Toc297378610}[]{#_Toc295469854}[]{#_Toc263067846}[]{#_Toc207010334}[]{#_Toc207010067}[]{#_Ref141094980}[]{#_Toc194978676}[]{#_Toc195531451}[]{#_Toc194978677}[]{#_Toc195531452}[]{#_Toc194978678}[]{#_Toc195531453}[]{#_Toc194978679}[]{#_Toc195531454}[]{#_Toc194978680}[]{#_Toc195531455}[]{#_Toc194978681}[]{#_Toc195531456}[]{#_Toc194978682}[]{#_Toc195531457}[]{#_Toc194978683}[]{#_Toc195531458}[]{#_Toc194978684}[]{#_Toc195531459}[]{#_Toc194978685}[]{#_Toc195531460}[]{#_Toc194978686}[]{#_Toc195531461}[]{#_Toc194978687}[]{#_Toc195531462}[]{#_Toc194978688}[]{#_Toc195531463}[]{#_Toc194978689}[]{#_Toc195531464}[]{#_Toc194978690}[]{#_Toc195531465}[]{#_Toc194978695}[]{#_Toc195531470}[]{#_Toc194978696}[]{#_Toc195531471}[]{#_Toc194978700}[]{#_Toc195531475}[]{#_Toc194978701}[]{#_Toc195531476}[]{#_Toc194978714}[]{#_Toc195531489}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- broadcast-suppression**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){#图片 12 width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2009060321}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_969947730}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1559554667}[命令用来开启端口广播风暴抑制功能，并设置广播风暴抑制阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x821276161}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1240733562}

[**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *ratio \|* **pps** *max-pps \|* **kbps** *max-kbps* }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1889652448}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **broadcast-suppression**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1534554634}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556435889}

[[所有接口不对广播流量进行抑制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1060609561}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1740823737}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1382980440}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1142060623}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1438563368}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_104411514}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2128550422}

[*[ratio]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x702689387}[：指定以太网接口允许通过的最大广播流量占该接口带宽的百分比，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[。数值越小，允许通过的广播流量也越小。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}***[ max-pps]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556370353}[：指定以太网接口每秒允许转发的最大广播包数，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[pps]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[packets per second]{lang="EN-US"}[，每秒转发的报文数），取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1.4881]{lang="EN-US"}[×接口带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[kbps ]{lang="EN-US"}***[max-kbps]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1478664508}[：指定以太网接口每秒允许转发的最大广播流量，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kilobits per second]{lang="EN-US"}[，每秒转发的千比特数），]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～接口带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_635897649}

[[本命令设置的是接口允许通过的最大广播报文流量。当接口上的广播流量超过用户设置的值后，系统将丢弃超出广播流量限制的报文，从而使接口广播流量所占的比例控制在限定的范围内，以便保证业务的正常运行。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1283673224}

[[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x199490925}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令都能开启端口的广播风暴抑制功能，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令通过软件对广播报文进行抑制，对设备性能有一定影响，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[通过芯片物理上对广播报文进行抑制，相对]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[来说，对设备性能影响较小。请不要同时配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，以免配置冲突，导致抑制效果不确定。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[当风暴抑制阈值配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_160638503}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}**[时，设备可能会根据芯片支持的步长，将配置值转换成步长的倍数。所以，端口下配置的抑制阈值可能与实际生效抑制阈值不一致，请注意查看设备的提示信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_568748816}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_452575775}[在以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上，每秒最多允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[广播报文通过，对超出该范围的广播报文进行抑制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556566961}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] broadcast-suppression kbps 10000]{lang="EN-US"}

[The actual value is 10048 on port GigabitEthernet1/0/1 currently.]{lang="EN-US"}

[[以上信息表示：用户配置的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_908684253}[，因为芯片支持的步长为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[64]{lang="EN-US"}[，所以实际生效的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10048kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[64]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[157]{lang="EN-US"}[倍）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1333587119}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1109182703}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[unicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1824012949}
:::::

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- display storm-constrain**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){#图片 14 width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1679420428}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_908932228}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[display storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x888689553}[命令用来显示接口流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556501425}

[**[display storm-constrain ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **broadcast** \| **multicast** \| **unicast** \] \[ **interface** *interface-type* *interface-number* \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2134512944}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1526992393}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1367999665}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1046159978}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x718512851}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1882057034}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1928276668}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x463641925}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556698033}

[**[broadcast]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1616200129}[：只显示广播报文流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[multicast]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x822213276}[：只显示组播报文流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[unicast]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x79274368}[：只显示未知单播报文流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[interface ]{lang="EN-US"}***[interface-type interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1343305602}**[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[显示指定接口的报文流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[interface-type interface-number]{lang="EN-US"}*[指定接口类型和接口编号。不指定该参数时，显示所有接口报文的流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x41771329}

[[不指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[broadcast]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2080339192}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[multicast]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[unicast]{lang="EN-US"}**[参数时，则显示所有类型报文的流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1876346344}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1596854003}[显示系统当前所有接口的流量控制信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[BC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556108206}[：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[broadcast]{lang="EN-US"}[，表示广播报文]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[       ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[MC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_38677499}[：]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[multicast]{lang="EN-US"}[，表示组播报文]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mdix-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_758900134}[命令用来设置以太网接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MDIX]{lang="EN-US"}[模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_576411351}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1199874168}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_970205719}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556239279}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556435887}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1060609547}

[**[automdix]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1740692663}[：两端设备通过协商来决定引脚]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[是发送还是接收信号，引脚]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[6]{lang="EN-US"}[是接收还是发送信号。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[mdi]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1494199418}[：使用引脚]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[发送信号，使用引脚]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[6]{lang="EN-US"}[接收信号。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[mdix]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1132149774}[：使用引脚]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2]{lang="EN-US"}[接收信号，使用引脚]{style="font-family:宋体"}[3]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[6]{lang="EN-US"}[发送信号。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_108938556}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1829944986}[设置以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MDIX]{lang="EN-US"}[模式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[mdi]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- multicast-suppression**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1478664510}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_636421936}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1180695094}[命令用来开启端口组播风暴抑制功能，并设置组播风暴抑制阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1364643205}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1841742734}

[**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *ratio \|* **pps** *max-pps* \| **kbps** *max-kbps* }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_573725250}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **multicast-suppression**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x528945778}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1681433576}

[[所有接口不对组播流量进行抑制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556566959}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_240390985}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1773481870}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_379371494}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2116148547}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x29022928}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x296285176}

[*[ratio]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1596818253}[：指定以太网接口允许通过的最大组播流量占该接口带宽的百分比。取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[。数值越小，则允许通过的组播流量也越小。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}***[ max-pps]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556501423}[：指定以太网接口每秒最多通过的组播包包数，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1.4881]{lang="EN-US"}[×接口带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[kbps ]{lang="EN-US"}***[max-kbps]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2134512950}[：指定以太网接口每秒最多通过的组播流量，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[单位为]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～接口带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1527254538}

[[本命令设置的是接口允许通过的最大组播报文流量。当接口上的组播流量超过用户设置的值后，系统将丢弃超出组播流量限制的报文，从而使接口组播流量所占的比例控制在限定的范围内，以便保证业务的正常运行。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1349687189}

[[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_996002229}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令都能开启端口的组播风暴抑制功能，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令通过软件对组播报文进行抑制，对设备性能有一定影响，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[通过芯片物理上对组播报文进行抑制，相对]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[来说，对设备性能影响较小。请不要同时配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，以免配置冲突，导致抑制效果不确定。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[当风暴抑制阈值配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x318809455}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}**[时，设备可能会根据芯片支持的步长，将配置值转换成步长的倍数。所以，端口下配置的抑制阈值可能与实际生效抑制阈值不一致，请注意查看设备的提示信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1329593511}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2063818680}[在以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上，每秒最多允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[组播报文通过，对超出该范围的组播报文进行抑制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556698031}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] multicast-suppression kbps 10000]{lang="EN-US"}

[The actual value is 10048 on port GigabitEthernet1/0/1 currently.]{lang="EN-US"}

[[以上信息表示：用户配置的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_908618717}[，因为芯片支持的步长为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[64]{lang="EN-US"}[，所以实际生效的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10048kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[64]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[157]{lang="EN-US"}[倍）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1616200127}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x822868636}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[unicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x101669195}[]{#_Toc206821191}[]{#_Toc207010095}[]{#_Toc207010362}[]{#_Toc207011430}[]{#_Toc207611128}
:::::

[]{#_Toc404783580}[]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x687577288}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x687904969}

[**[extend]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x687577289}[：设置端口工作在扩展连接模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[normal]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1902206301}[：设置端口工作在正常连接模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x687511753}

[[当设备用于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OAA]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x688101578}[组网环境时，需要分别在设备和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OAP]{lang="EN-US"}[插卡上，将设备和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OAP]{lang="EN-US"}[插卡的内联接口的连接模式配置为扩展连接模式（]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[extend]{lang="EN-US"}**[），才能够实现设备和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OAP]{lang="EN-US"}[插卡间的通信。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1304577794}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x688036042}[设置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}[接口的连接模式为扩展连接模式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x688232650}

[\<Sysname\> interface ten-gigabitethernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-]{lang="EN-US"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/1]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt"}[\] port connection-mode extend]{lang="EN-US"}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- port up-mode**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image003.png){#图片 3 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x774914643}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1784490677}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[port up-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x932763938}[命令用来强制开启光口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo port up-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_556632495}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2051741009}

[**[port up-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1759206442}

[**[undo port up-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1208168834}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x589004289}

[[没有强制开启光口，光口的物理状态由光纤的物理状态决定。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x589463041}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1700738910}

[[以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1288765726}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_918928922}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1870961411}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1602649569}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122257687}

[[强制开启光口后，不管实际的光纤链路是否连通，甚至没有插入光纤或光模块，光口的物理状态都会变为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_398766730}[。此时，只要光口上有一条光纤链路是连通的，就可以实现报文的单向转发，以达到节约传输链路的效果。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_756337923}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[仅]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1162086824}[GE]{lang="EN-US"}[光口和工作在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LAN]{lang="EN-US"}[模式下的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10GE]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[40GE]{lang="EN-US"}[光口支持强制开启功能，电口和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Combo]{lang="EN-US"}[口不支持该功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[光口必须工作在二层模式下才支持强制开启功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_607588208}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[port up-mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1921515080}[和]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令互斥，不能同时配置。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[光口被强制开启后，光口的物理状态始终为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_290125464}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[，不受光纤]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[光模块拔插的影响。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[光口被强制开启后，]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1835144817}[GE]{lang="EN-US"}[光口插入光电转换模块、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100/1000M]{lang="EN-US"}[光模块、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100M]{lang="EN-US"}[光模块后，流量不能正常转发。必须取消强制开启光口配置，才能正常转发。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122192151}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1587015633}[强制开启光口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x924690405}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] port up-mode]{lang="EN-US"}

font-family:Symbol"}[本命令视图的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x964677243}

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[port-type]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_968380871}[命令用来在二层以太网接口和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}[接口间进行类型切换。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122388759}

[[在二层以太网接口视图下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1526057871}

[**[port-type]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **fc**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x480723125}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1328402548}[接口视图下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[port-type]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **ethernet**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1337582450}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1574522885}

[[接口为二层以太网接口类型。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1950582552}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_907885365}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/FC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1409759179}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122323223}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x786201457}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1372633311}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_970959199}

[**[ethernet]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1361398272}[：将当前接口切换到二层以太网接口类型，切换后的接口速率与设备型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[fc]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_816373357}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[将当前接口切换到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}[接口类型]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1766312732}

[[某些二层以太网接口支持切换到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x880067295}[接口。如果要将这些二层以太网接口切换为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}[接口，则需要进入对应的二层以太网接口视图执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port-type]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **fc**]{lang="EN-US"}[命令；如果要将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}[接口切换回二层以太网接口，则需要进入对应的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[port-type]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **ethernet**]{lang="EN-US"}[命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[接口类型切换后，原接口删除并创建新的接口，切换后的接口编号与切换前保持一致。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1707385294}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122519831}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_779567346}[将二层以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Ten-GigabitEthernet1/1/5]{lang="EN-US"}[切换为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FC]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- speed auto**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image005.png){#图片 54 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1365389163}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号以及具体的接口类型有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_662345972}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[speed auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_932261607}[命令用来设置以太网接口的自协商速率范围。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo speed]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122454295}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1566594416}

[**[speed auto]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { **10** \| **100** \| **1000** } \*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1984243091}

[**[undo speed]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_94634768}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1207500949}

[[本命令的缺省情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_89269059}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_794543906}

[[百兆或者千兆二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x709410087}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122650903}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_24586145}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_432620169}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x799173709}

[**[10]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1770338352}[：设置接口自协商速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[100]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1564380301}[：设置接口自协商速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[1000]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_580670446}[：设置接口自协商速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，本参数的支持情况与业务板的型号有关，请以业务板的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x448207369}

[[如果多次使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1203095967}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed auto]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令设置接口的速率，则最新配置生效。例如：若在接口下先配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed auto 100 1000]{lang="EN-US"}**[，然后又配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed 100]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则接口的速率强制为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，不进行协商；若在接口下先配置了]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed 100]{lang="EN-US"}**[，然后又配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed auto 100 1000]{lang="EN-US"}**[，则接口将与对端协商速率，协商的结果只能为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122585367}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果两端使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1957574221}[命令用来设置接口自协商速率的范围完全不同，例如：一端为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[speed auto 10 100]{lang="EN-US"}[，另一端为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[speed auto 1000]{lang="EN-US"}[，此时两端速率协商不成功；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果两端使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x842703604}[命令用来设置接口自协商速率的范围部分相同，例如：一端为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[speed auto 10 100]{lang="EN-US"}[，另一端为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[speed auto 100 1000]{lang="EN-US"}[，此时两端速率协商为双方都有的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[100 Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[；]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果两端使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed auto]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_837131478}[命令用来设置接口自协商速率的范围完全相同，例如：一端为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[speed auto 100 1000]{lang="EN-US"}**[，另一端为]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}**[speed auto 100 1000]{lang="EN-US"}**[，此时两端取速率协商范围内最大速率]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[1000 Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x57663131}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_210734630}[设置接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的自协商速率为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1000Mbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x737869828}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] speed auto 10 1000]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_562434901}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[speed]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122781975}
:::::

::::: {#-970937471 .myid}
[]{#_Toc404783584}[]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1543529504}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- storm-constrain**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image004.png){#图片 21 width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2024700236}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1236093601}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1768489637}[命令用来开启端口流量阈值控制功能，并设置上限阈值与下限阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x844846396}[命令用来取消端口报文流量上限阈值配置及下限阈值配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1618127305}

[**[storm-constrain ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **broadcast** \| **multicast** \| **unicast** } { **pps** \| **kbps** \| **ratio** } *upperlimit lowerlimit*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1189516329}

[**[undo storm-constrain ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **all** \| **broadcast** \| **multicast** \| **unicast** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122716439}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1590771211}

[[没有设置端口的流量阈值，即不对端口的报文流量进行抑制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x287186001}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_303836573}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2001353424}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2965729}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x939237837}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_591365149}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_124092483}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x811634622}[：取消端口所有类型（未知单播、组播和广播）报文流量阈值配置。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[broadcast]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122257688}[：设置端口广播报文流量阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[multicast]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_399487626}[：设置端口组播报文流量阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[unicast]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_616118259}[：设置端口未知单播报文流量阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1515790115}[：以包每秒为单位统计流量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x777714494}[：以千比特每秒为单位统计流量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[ratio]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1879255788}[：以每秒钟报文所占接口物理带宽的百分比来统计流量。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[upperlimit]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1904387285}[：端口报文流量的上限阈值。当和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}**[一起使用时，该参数的取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1.4881]{lang="EN-US"}[×接口带宽；当和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}**[一起使用时，该参数的取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～接口带宽；当和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ratio]{lang="EN-US"}**[一起使用时，该参数的取值范围为为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[lowerlimit]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x492207658}[：端口报文流量的下限阈值。当和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}**[一起使用时，该参数的取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1.4881]{lang="EN-US"}[×接口带宽；当和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}**[一起使用时，该参数的取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～接口带宽；当和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ratio]{lang="EN-US"}**[一起使用时，该参数的取值范围为为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1347131929}

[[执行本命令后，设备就会周期性地对接口收到的指定类型的报文进行统计，如果流量超过上限阈值，则采取一定的措施。其中，通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122192152}[命令可以配置统计周期，通过]{style="font-family:
宋体"}**[storm-constrain control]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令可以配置流量超过上限阈值时采取的控制方式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1586819025}[与]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[unitcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令都能开启端口的风暴抑制功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令通过软件对报文流量进行抑制，对设备性能有一定影响，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[unitcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[通过芯片物理上对报文流量进行抑制，相对]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[来说，对设备性能影响较小。对于某种类型的报文流量，请不要同时配置这两种方式，以免配置冲突，导致抑制效果不确定。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[配置时，]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[upperlimit]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1385938574}[值必须大于]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[lowerlimit]{lang="EN-US"}*[值，建议不要配成相等值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1915401969}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_62427140}[对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[端口配置未知单播流量阈值，上限阈值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[200pps]{lang="EN-US"}[、下限阈值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[150pps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_720330945}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] storm-constrain unicast pps 200 150]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x84728965}[对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/2]{lang="EN-US"}[端口配置广播流量阈值，上限阈值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[、下限阈值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1500kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1363727846}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/2]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/2\] storm-constrain broadcast kbps 2000 1500]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122388760}[对]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}[端口配置组播流量百分比阈值，上限为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[80%]{lang="EN-US"}[、下限为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[15%]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1525468050}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/3]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/3\] storm-constrain multicast ratio 80 15]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_365746224}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[storm-constrain control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x182307872}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[storm-constrain interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_133997153}
:::::

[]{#_Toc404783585}[]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_770875224}[]{#_Toc297726934}[]{#_Toc291763736}[]{#_Toc263067870}[]{#_Toc207010354}[]{#_Toc207010087}[]{#_Toc156034686}[]{#_Toc155696117}[]{#_Toc151989535}[]{#_Toc98822460}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- storm-constrain control**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){#图片 22 width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1341120412}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1984635875}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[storm-constrain control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122323224}[命令用来设置端口未知单播、组播或者广播流量超过上限阈值时采取的控制方式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo storm-constrain control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x786660209}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1758247637}

[**[storm-constrain control ]{lang="EN-US"}**[{ **block** \| **shutdown** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_354380160}

[**[undo storm-constrain control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1740460410}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1432534122}

[[不对端口流量进行任何控制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_465060362}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x981224704}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1607854412}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122519832}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_779763954}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1935989623}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1999015610}

[**[block]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x569641094}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[block]{lang="EN-US"}[方式，即：当端口上未知单播、组播或广播报文中某类报文的流量大于其上限阈值时，端口将暂停转发该类报文（其它类型报文照常转发），端口处于阻塞状态，但仍会统计该类报文的流量。当该类报文的流量小于其下限阈值时，端口将自动恢复对此类报文的转发。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1259432407}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}[方式，即：当端口上未知单播、组播或广播报文中某类报文的流量大于其上限阈值时，端口将被关闭，系统停止转发所有报文。当该类报文的流量小于其下限阈值时，端口状态不会自动恢复，此时可通过执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[undo shutdown]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令或取消端口上流量阈值的配置来恢复。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1493969519}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x921519106}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[端口，当流量超过上限阈值时，采用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[block]{lang="EN-US"}[方式控制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122454296}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] storm-constrain control block]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1566791024}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1622722563}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[storm-constrain control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1752702126}

宋体"}[Log]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- storm-constrain enable trap**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image003.png){#图片 8 width="62" height="25"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1545651058}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122781976}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[storm-constrain enable trap]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1543726112}[命令用来配置端口流量大于上限阈值或者小于下限阈值时输出]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[Trap]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo storm-constrain enable trap]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x93282950}[命令用来禁止端口流量大于上限阈值或者小于下限阈值时输出]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Trap]{lang="EN-US"}[信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo storm-constrain enable trap]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_982918786}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_674028848}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- storm-constrain interval**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){#图片 23 width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_398635658}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1233692723}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[storm-constrain interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x743774733}[命令用来配置端口流量阈值控制模块流量统计的时间间隔。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[**[undo storm-constrain interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x700432639}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_791480366}

[**[storm-constrain interval]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *seconds*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1926459688}

[**[undo storm-constrain interval]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x801298237}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122192149}

[[端口流量统计的时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1586491344}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x131636606}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x496633441}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_697288858}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1703179178}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_597048642}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1353588693}

[*[seconds]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_639211703}[：端口流量统计的时间间隔，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[300]{lang="EN-US"}[，单位为秒。为了保持网络状态的稳定，建议设置的时间间隔不低于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122388757}

[[本命令设置的时间间隔专门为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1525664655}[命令服务的，不同于]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[flow-interval]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令设置的时间间隔。虽然同样是统计端口流量，但是功能是分开的。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1169268434}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1993342604}[配置端口流量统计时间间隔为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[60]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x432050278}

[\[Sysname\] storm-constrain interval 60]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_846913964}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1802420933}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[storm-constrain control]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1334333751}
:::::

[]{#_Toc404783589}[]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122323221}[]{#_Toc297378612}[]{#_Toc295469856}[]{#_Toc263067874}[]{#_Toc207010359}[]{#_Toc207010092}[]{#_Ref141175906}

**以太网接口 \-- 二层以太网接口的配置命令 \-- unicast-suppression**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

::: {style="border:none;border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:1.0pt 0cm 0cm 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[![说明](以太网接口命令.files/image002.png){#图片 24 width="63" height="26"}]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x786332529}
:::

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;
margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x190555280}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[unicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1992653857}[命令用来开启端口未知单播风暴抑制功能，并设置未知单播风暴抑制阈值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo unicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1687865860}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1174781302}

[**[unicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[ { *ratio* \| **pps** *max-pps* \| **kbps** *max-kbps* }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x552681461}

[**[undo]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **unicast-suppression**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_744259121}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1965137481}

[[所有接口不对未知单播流量进行抑制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122519829}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_779043059}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x55886277}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1138690517}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1076489000}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1115575112}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x884448684}

[*[ratio]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1235902438}[：指定以太网接口最大未知单播流量占该接口带宽的百分比。取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[100]{lang="EN-US"}[。数值越小，则允许通过的未知单播流量也越小。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}***[ max-pps]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122454293}[：指定以太网接口每秒最多通过的未知单播包包数，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1.4881]{lang="EN-US"}[×接口带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[kbps ]{lang="EN-US"}***[max-kbps]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1566987632}[：指定以太网接口每秒通过的未知单播流量，单位为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～接口带宽。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1961263842}

[[本命令设置的是接口允许通过的最大未知单播报文流量。当接口上的未知单播流量超过用户设置的值后，系统将丢弃超出未知单播流量限制的报文，从而使接口未知单播流量所占的比例降低到限定的范围，保证网络业务的正常运行。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2035397091}

[[执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[unicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x2115196371}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令都能开启端口的未知单播风暴抑制功能，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令通过软件对未知单播报文进行抑制，对设备性能有一定影响，]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[unitcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[通过芯片物理上对未知单播报文进行抑制，相对]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[来说，对设备性能影响较小。请不要同时配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[unitcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[storm-constrain]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令，以免配置冲突，导致抑制效果不确定。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[当风暴抑制阈值配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[pps]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x731672304}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[kbps]{lang="EN-US"}**[时，设备可能会根据芯片支持的步长，将配置值转换成步长的倍数。所以，端口下配置的抑制阈值可能与实际生效抑制阈值不一致，请注意查看设备的提示信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x188822518}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x679584713}[在以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上，每秒最多允许]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[未知单播报文通过，对超出该范围的未知单播报文进行抑制。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122650901}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] unicast-suppression kbps 10000]{lang="EN-US"}

[The actual value is 10048 on port GigabitEthernet1/0/1 currently.]{lang="EN-US"}

[[以上信息表示：用户配置的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10000kbps]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1013892192}[，因为芯片支持的步长为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[64]{lang="EN-US"}[，所以实际生效的值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[10048kbps]{lang="EN-US"}[（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[64]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[157]{lang="EN-US"}[倍）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_24455073}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[broadcast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1377496244}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[multicast-suppression]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x64360415}
:::::

[]{#_Toc404783590}[]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x858964184}[]{#_Toc315959348}[]{#_Toc307476938}[]{#_Toc284246800}[]{#_Toc263067876}[]{#_Toc207010360}[]{#_Toc207010093}[]{#_Toc139515349}[]{#_Toc137103182}

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[virtual-cable-test]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2093440328}[命令用来对以太网接口连接电缆进行一次检测，并显示检测结果。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122585365}

[**[virtual-cable-test]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1957443149}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1184716638}

[[二层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x422158262}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x100162137}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_416745384}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1097243938}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_190970651}

[[在以太网接口上执行本命令会使已经]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x11727680}[的链路自动]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[down]{lang="EN-US"}[一次。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[检测结果仅供参考，检测到的长度最大误差为]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122781973}[5]{lang="EN-US"}[米。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果显示值为"]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x687970509}[-]{lang="EN-US"}["，则表示不支持该项参数的检测。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1543922720}

[*[mac-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1415267024}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，形式为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[H-H-H]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[给子接口配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x344386014}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址时，请不要使用]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VRRP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议保留的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MAC]{lang="EN-US"}[地址段。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**以太网接口 \-- 三层以太网接口/子接口的配置命令 \-- mtu**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x253811868}[命令用来设置三层以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[子接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[值。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1137081968}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1701207930}

[**[mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *size*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1806314810}

[**[undo mtu]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122781974}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1543595040}

[[三层以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1507815562}[子接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1500]{lang="EN-US"}[字节。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1510159179}

[[三层以太网接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_730919627}[三层以太网子接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1484983430}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_423474683}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1834936508}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x723367509}

[*[size]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122716438}[：以太网接口允许通过的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[的大小，取值范围取决于接口的类型，单位为字节。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1590836747}

[[由于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[QoS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_874463454}[队列长度的限制（如]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIFO]{lang="EN-US"}[队列的缺省长度为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[75]{lang="EN-US"}[），]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[太小会造成分片太多，从而被]{style="font-family:宋体"}[QoS]{lang="EN-US"}[队列丢弃。此时，可适当增大]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MTU]{lang="EN-US"}[值或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[QoS]{lang="EN-US"}[队列的长度。以太网接口视图下的命令]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[qos fifo queue-length]{lang="EN-US"}**[可以改变]{style="font-family:宋体"}[QoS]{lang="EN-US"}[队列长度（具体配置请参见"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[QoS]{lang="EN-US"}[配置指导"中的"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[QoS]{lang="EN-US"}["）。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x444429620}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1691866429}[设置三层以太网接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[的最大传输单元为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1430Bytes]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2143806573}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1\] mtu 1430]{lang="EN-US"}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1035568462}[设置三层以太网子接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1.1]{lang="EN-US"}[的最大传输单元为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1430Bytes]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_2122257683}

[\[Sysname\] interface gigabitethernet 1/0/1.1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet1/0/1.1\] mtu 1430]{lang="EN-US"}

margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}

::: {style="border:none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;padding:0cm 0cm 1.0pt 0cm;

[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:楷体_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1233908701}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[port-type switch]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1855010900}[命令用来在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[POS]{lang="EN-US"}[接口和三层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口间进行类型切换。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1784855293}

[[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[POS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x263408590}[接口视图下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[port-type switch gigabitethernet]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1881373811}

[[在三层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GE]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x974454021}[接口视图下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[port-type switch pos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1241197331}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1784789757}

[[POS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1998643486}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/]{lang="EN-US"}[三层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_504011800}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x754854005}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1784724221}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x506701853}

[**[gigabitethernet]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_962094626}[：将当前]{style="font-family:宋体"}[POS]{lang="EN-US"}[接口切换为三层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[pos]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1499359648}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[将当前三层]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GE]{lang="EN-US"}[接口切换为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[POS]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1751334745}

[[接口类型切换后，原接口删除并创建新的接口，切换后的接口编号与切换前保持一致。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1784658685}

[[命令执行成功后会自动切换到新接口的接口视图下。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1407830784}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_1559110378}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x78109450}[将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[POS]{lang="EN-US"}[接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2/2/0]{lang="EN-US"}[切换为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet2/2/0]{lang="EN-US"}[接口。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_18460_x1527_x1784068861}

[\[Sysname\] interface pos 2/2/0]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-Pos2/2/0\] port-type switch gigabitethernet]{lang="EN-US"}

[Changing port type can result in loss of port configuration. Are you sure to continue? \[Y/N\]:y]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-GigabitEthernet2/2/0\]]{lang="EN-US"}
